`method asset::HostMemoryBackend::SetInUseQuery` — defined in `engine/asset/memory_backend.h`

```cpp
void SetInUseQuery(const ResourceInUseQuery& query);
```

Installs the dependents query (or clears it with a default-constructed one).

### `Add(ResourceId,ByteSpan,const MemoryAddOptions&)` {#asset-hostmemorybackend-add-resourceid-bytespan-const-memoryaddoptions-amp}

`method asset::HostMemoryBackend::Add` — defined in `engine/asset/memory_backend.h`

```cpp
Status Add(ResourceId id, ByteSpan bytes, const MemoryAddOptions& options = MemoryAddOptions());
```

Registers `bytes`, copied or, with `options.release`, lent.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `Invalid` (null id, a malformed path); `DuplicateId` (the id is live, or a path belongs to another id); `Corrupt` (not a blob, the blob's id is not `id`, the hash does not verify or differs from the host's); `UnsupportedVersion`, `TypeMismatch` (as `BlobReader::Open`); `LimitExceeded` (the budget); `Other` (memory). |

### `AddMany(Span<const MemoryAddItem>,usize*)` {#asset-hostmemorybackend-addmany-span-lt-const-memoryadditem-gt-usize-star}

`method asset::HostMemoryBackend::AddMany` — defined in `engine/asset/memory_backend.h`

```cpp
Status AddMany(Span<const MemoryAddItem> items, usize* failed_index = nullptr);
```

Adds every item, or none: the blobs are all checked, the ids and paths (also among the items) found free and the sum of the sizes found to fit before the first one is installed. The bytes are copied. On failure `*failed_index` (may be null) names the item that was refused.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | the failures of `Add` for each item; `DuplicateId` also for an id or path the batch names twice; `Invalid` for an item with a `release` function; `LimitExceeded` when the sum does not fit. |
| Ownership | borrows its arguments for the call only; the bytes are copied. |

### `Replace(ResourceId,ByteSpan,bool,Vector<ResourceId>*,const MemoryAddOptions&)` {#asset-hostmemorybackend-replace-resourceid-bytespan-bool-vector-lt-resourceid-gt-star-const-memoryaddoptions-amp}

`method asset::HostMemoryBackend::Replace` — defined in `engine/asset/memory_backend.h`

```cpp
Status Replace(ResourceId id, ByteSpan bytes, bool force = false, Vector<ResourceId>* dependents = nullptr, const MemoryAddOptions& options = MemoryAddOptions());
```

Replaces the bytes of a live resource with `bytes`, copied or lent as `options` says. Its paths stay unless `options` names new ones.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `NotFound`; `InUse` (dependents named in `*dependents` when it is not null; `force` unloads them first); the failures of `Add` for the new bytes. |
| Ownership | borrows its pointer arguments for the call only; nothing is retained. |

### `Remove(ResourceId,bool,Vector<ResourceId>*)` {#asset-hostmemorybackend-remove-resourceid-bool-vector-lt-resourceid-gt-star}

`method asset::HostMemoryBackend::Remove` — defined in `engine/asset/memory_backend.h`

```cpp
Status Remove(ResourceId id, bool force = false, Vector<ResourceId>* dependents = nullptr);
```

Removes a resource and its paths.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `NotFound`; `InUse` (dependents named in `*dependents` when it is not null; `force` unloads them first). |
| Ownership | borrows its pointer arguments for the call only; nothing is retained. |

## `QuantizeSkinInfluences(const u32*,const f64*,usize,MeshSkinInfluence*)` {#asset-quantizeskininfluences-const-u32-star-const-f64-star-usize-meshskininfluence-star}

`function asset::QuantizeSkinInfluences` — defined in `engine/asset/mesh.h`

```cpp
bool QuantizeSkinInfluences(const u32* joints, const f64* weights, usize count, MeshSkinInfluence* out);
```

Packs a source's up-to-four influences into quantized ones: the four largest weights (ties by joint index), weights renormalized in `f64` and rounded by the largest-remainder rule to sum to exactly 255, sorted by weight then joint.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `joints` | the influenced joints (duplicates are summed first by the caller). |
| Parameter `weights` | their non-negative finite weights, at least one positive. |
| Parameter `count` | the number of pairs. |
| Returns | false when no weight is positive. |
| Fails | reported by the return value. |
| Ownership | borrows the arrays and `out` for the call. |

## `EncodeMeshColor(const Array<f32,4>&)` {#asset-encodemeshcolor-const-array-lt-f32-4-gt-amp}

`function asset::EncodeMeshColor` — defined in `engine/asset/mesh.h`

```cpp
Array<u8, 4> EncodeMeshColor(const Array<f32, 4>& rgba);
```

Encodes a linear colour as a mesh stores it: rgb to sRGB, alpha linear, each rounded to 8 bits (the inverse of the shader's `SrgbToLinear`). Values outside 0..1 are clamped.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `rgba` | the linear colour. |
| Returns | the four bytes. |
| Fails | never; a value that is not a number encodes as 0. |

## `BuildMeshBlob(const TypeRegistry&,ResourceId,const MeshBuildInput&,Vector<byte>*,u32)` {#asset-buildmeshblob-const-typeregistry-amp-resourceid-const-meshbuildinput-amp-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-u32}

`function asset::BuildMeshBlob` — defined in `engine/asset/mesh.h`

```cpp
Status BuildMeshBlob(const TypeRegistry& types, ResourceId id, const MeshBuildInput& input, Vector<byte>* out, u32 target = 0);
```

Builds a mesh blob: packs the attributes, computes the bounds (and the joint boxes of a skinned mesh), records the measured packing error, and writes the sections.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `Invalid` (no vertices or indices, an index count that is not a multiple of 3, an index past the vertices, a non-finite value, a LOD or slot range outside the indices or not a multiple of 3, LOD screen sizes that grow, a slot name that is empty or too long, a morph target with a bad name, a delta past the vertices or out of order, more targets than [`asset::kMaxMorphTargets`](#asset-kmaxmorphtargets), a colour array of the wrong length, a skin array of the wrong length, a skin joint at or past the skeleton's joint count or weights that do not sum to 255); `LimitExceeded` for a count above the limits. |
| Ownership | borrows `input` for the call; `*out` is replaced. |

## `MeshColliderTriangleHasArea(const Array<f32,3>&,const Array<f32,3>&,const Array<f32,3>&)` {#asset-meshcollidertrianglehasarea-const-array-lt-f32-3-gt-amp-const-array-lt-f32-3-gt-amp-const-array-lt-f32-3-gt-amp}

`function asset::MeshColliderTriangleHasArea` — defined in `engine/asset/mesh_collider.h`

```cpp
bool MeshColliderTriangleHasArea(const Array<f32, 3>& a, const Array<f32, 3>& b, const Array<f32, 3>& c);
```

Tells whether a triangle has an area, by the physics module's own rule.

The cross product of `b - a` and `c - a` in `f32`, its squared length finite and above `1e-20`: exactly `physics::TriangleMesh::Create`'s test, so a triangle this accepts never makes a physics mesh refuse the collider.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `a` | the first corner. |
| Parameter `b` | the second corner. |
| Parameter `c` | the third corner. |
| Fails | never. |

| Ownership | the inputs are read during the call only; `out` is the caller's. |

## `MountId` {#asset-mountid}

## `MountTable` {#asset-mounttable}

`class asset::MountTable` — defined in `engine/asset/mount_table.h`

```cpp
class MountTable { ... }
```

Ordered mounts and the merged table.

A mount has a path prefix (`/` for none; the entries of its table appear under it), a priority (higher wins; the later of two equal priorities wins) and a backend the caller owns. Loads by id never use a table. Path questions (`Resolve`, `PathsOf`) are answered from the mounts' tables in priority order: a mount whose table has not arrived answers `NotReady` (never `NotFound`, which would be a lie), a mount whose table read failed answers with that failure, and a path two mounts map to different ids is won by the higher priority and listed as shadowed in the merged table.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Thread-safety | thread-safe. The tables-ready callback runs on the thread that finished the last table read. A backend must not be unmounted (or destroyed) while a call into it from `Stat` or `Open` is running. |
| Ownership | borrows the backends (they must outlive their mount); owns its tickets and tables. |

### `MountTable()` {#asset-mounttable-mounttable}

`constructor asset::MountTable::MountTable` — defined in `engine/asset/mount_table.h`

```cpp
MountTable() = default;
```

_Defaulted._

`constructor asset::MountTable::MountTable` — defined in `engine/asset/mount_table.h`

```cpp

```

_Deleted._

`method asset::MountTable::Mount` — defined in `engine/asset/mount_table.h`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `Invalid` (a null backend, a prefix that is not a valid path or `/`). |
| Ownership | borrows `backend`, which must outlive the mount; `*out` is written on success only. |

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `NotFound`. |

`method asset::MountTable::Stat` — defined in `engine/asset/mount_table.h`

```cpp

```

Describes `id` from the highest-priority backend that has it.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `NotFound` when no mount has it; any other failure of a backend that has it is returned as it is (a corrupt resource is not silently replaced by a lower mount's copy). |
| Ownership | borrows its pointer arguments for the call only; nothing is retained. |

### `RefreshTablesAsync()` {#asset-mounttable-refreshtablesasync}

`method asset::MountTable::RefreshTablesAsync` — defined in `engine/asset/mount_table.h`

```cpp
void RefreshTablesAsync();
```

Starts every mount's `ReadTableAsync` without blocking; each table is merged as it arrives. A read still running from an earlier call is cancelled and replaced.

### `RefreshTables()` {#asset-mounttable-refreshtables}

`method asset::MountTable::RefreshTables` — defined in `engine/asset/mount_table.h`


## `RegisterNetworkSettings(settings::Registry*)` {#asset-registernetworksettings-settings-registry-star}

`function asset::RegisterNetworkSettings` — defined in `engine/asset/network_backend.h`

```cpp
void RegisterNetworkSettings(settings::Registry* registry);
```

Declares the settings of the network backend: `assets.net.timeout_ms` (how long a request, a connection or a handshake may take), `assets.net.max_retries` (attempts after the first before the backend is `Offline`), `assets.net.backoff_ms` (the first delay between attempts, doubled each time up to 32 times), `assets.net.prefetch_bytes` (resources up to this size are fetched whole), `assets.net.cache_bytes` (the most bytes of blobs the disk cache holds) and `assets.cache_dir` (the cache directory; empty for no disk cache). A key that is already declared is left as it is.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Thread-safety | thread-compatible; call it while the registry is being set up. |
| Ownership | borrows its pointer arguments for the call only; nothing is retained. |

## `PakWriter` {#asset-pakwriter}

`class asset::PakWriter` — defined in `engine/asset/pak.h`

```cpp
class PakWriter { ... }
```

Builds a pak. Adds borrow the blobs; `Finish` writes the file deterministically (entries in id order, zero padding, no time stamps), so the same inputs give the same bytes.

The writer is an enforcement point: a blob that is not the current version of its type, whose own id differs from the id it is added under, whose hash does not verify or that was laid out for another target is refused, and `Finish` refuses a pak whose closure is incomplete.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Thread-safety | thread-compatible. |
| Ownership | borrows every added blob and the registry until `Finish` returns. |

### `PakWriter(const TypeRegistry&,const PakWriterOptions&)` {#asset-pakwriter-pakwriter-const-typeregistry-amp-const-pakwriteroptions-amp}

`constructor asset::PakWriter::PakWriter` — defined in `engine/asset/pak.h`

```cpp

```

Makes a writer that checks blobs against `types`.

`method asset::PakWriter::SetBase` — defined in `engine/asset/pak.h`

```cpp

```

Names a base pak (a patch pak): dependencies may be satisfied by it, and its identity goes into the header.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Ownership | borrows `base` until `Finish` returns. |

### `Add(ResourceId,ByteSpan,StringView)` {#asset-pakwriter-add-resourceid-bytespan-stringview}

`method asset::PakWriter::Add` — defined in `engine/asset/pak.h`

```cpp
Status Add(ResourceId id, ByteSpan blob, StringView path = StringView());
```

Adds a blob, optionally with its path.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `Corrupt`, `UnsupportedVersion`, `TypeMismatch` (as `BlobReader::Open` and `Verify`); `Corrupt` when the blob's own id is not `id`; `WrongTarget`; `DuplicateId`; `Invalid` or `DuplicateId` for a path; `Invalid` for the null id; `LimitExceeded` past `kMaxPakEntries`. |
| Ownership | borrows `blob` until `Finish` returns. |

```cpp
class PakBackend : public ResourceBackend { ... }
```

Serves the entries of one pak by id.

A patch pak names its base pak by identity (the base's index hash); `Stat`, `Open` and `ReadTable` of a patch pak fail with `MissingDependency` until the base's backend is linked with `LinkBase`. `Watch` reports nothing.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Thread-safety | thread-safe once opened; `LinkBase` belongs to setup, before the first read. |
| Ownership | owns the reader; readers it hands out must not outlive it. |


## `NormalizePath(StringView,String<HeapAllocator>*)` {#asset-normalizepath-stringview-string-lt-heapallocator-gt-star}

`function asset::NormalizePath` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_id.h`

```cpp
Status NormalizePath(StringView path, String<HeapAllocator>* out);
```

Normalizes a virtual path into its one canonical spelling: UTF-8 in Unicode NFC, a leading `/`, segments separated by single `/`, no `.` or `..` segment, no trailing slash, no empty segment, no backslash and no control character, at most [`asset::kMaxPathBytes`](#asset-kmaxpathbytes) bytes (and [`asset::kMaxPathSegmentBytes`](#asset-kmaxpathsegmentbytes) per segment). Case is significant. The root `/` alone is not a resource path. A path that cannot be made canonical is an error, never repaired by guesswork.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `path` | the path as written. |
| Parameter `out` | receives the canonical spelling on success; may be null to only check. |
| Fails | `Invalid` with a message naming the broken rule; `TooLarge` above the length limits. |
| Ownership | `out` receives a new string from the default heap allocator; `path` is not kept. |

One blob read into a pooled buffer, shared by everything that views it: the manager's slot, the loaded object, and the GPU objects uploaded from it (a mesh that is uploaded again after a device reset reads these bytes). ## `ResourceManager` {#asset-resourcemanager}

`class asset::ResourceManager` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_manager.h`

```cpp
class ResourceManager { ... }
```

The manager. See the file comment.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Thread-safety | thread-safe. One lock guards the loaded set; a load holds it while it reads, so loads are serialized (asynchronous loading is separate). A loader must not call back into the manager. |
| Ownership | borrows the type registry and the backends given to `Mount`; owns the backends given to `MountPath` and `MountOwned`, the pool, the loaded resources and the loaders. Handles must be gone before the manager. |

### `RegisterFallback(ResourceType,ByteSpan)` {#asset-resourcemanager-registerfallback-resourcetype-bytespan}

`method asset::ResourceManager::RegisterFallback` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_manager.h`

```cpp
Status RegisterFallback(ResourceType type, ByteSpan blob);
```

Registers the fallback of a resource type: a blob (copied) that stands in for a resource of that type that is missing or invalid, when `assets.allow_fallbacks` is on. Each use is logged and counted; a fallback never stands in for a dependency (a closure with a missing member fails as a whole).

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `type` | the type. |
| Parameter `blob` | a valid blob of the type. |
| Fails | `Invalid` for a blob that does not open as a blob of `type`. |

### `Load(ResourceId,Handle<T>*,LoadFailure*)` {#asset-resourcemanager-load-resourceid-handle-lt-t-gt-star-loadfailure-star}

`method asset::ResourceManager::Load` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_manager.h`

```cpp
template <class T>
Status Load(ResourceId id, Handle<T>* out, LoadFailure* failure = nullptr);
```

Loads the resource `id` and its closure, or finds it loaded.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `id` | the resource. |
| Parameter `out` | receives the handle on success; reset on failure. |
| Parameter `failure` | receives where a failure stopped when not null. |
| Fails | `NotFound` (no mount has it); `MissingDependency` (a dependency is nowhere; the chain is in `failure`); `DependencyTypeMismatch` (a dependency is not of the declared type or version, or a loader found the closure inconsistent); `DependencyCycle`; `UnsupportedVersion` (counted in `rejected_old_version`); `Corrupt`; `TypeMismatch` (the blob is not of the type of `T`, or `T` is not what the type's loader makes); `LimitExceeded` (`assets.cache_bytes`, or the buffer pool's budget `assets.buffer_pool_bytes`, which the resident blobs occupy); `TypeMismatch` also when no loader is registered for the type. With `assets.allow_fallbacks` and a registered fallback, `NotFound`, `MissingDependency`, `Corrupt`, `UnsupportedVersion` and `DependencyTypeMismatch` return the fallback instead; the others never do. |
| Ownership | `*out` receives the handle, which owns one reference; `*failure` is written when not null. |

### `LoadPath(StringView,Handle<T>*,LoadFailure*)` {#asset-resourcemanager-loadpath-stringview-handle-lt-t-gt-star-loadfailure-star}

`method asset::ResourceManager::LoadPath` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_manager.h`

```cpp
template <class T>
Status LoadPath(StringView path, Handle<T>* out, LoadFailure* failure = nullptr);
```

Loads the resource a path names (through the mounts' tables).

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `NotReady` (a table has not arrived), `NotFound`, `Invalid` (the path), and those of `Load`. |

### `ReadBlob(ResourceId,BlobBufferPtr*)` {#asset-resourcemanager-readblob-resourceid-blobbufferptr-star}

`method asset::ResourceManager::ReadBlob` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_manager.h`

```cpp
Status ReadBlob(ResourceId id, BlobBufferPtr* out);
```

Reads the blob of `id` into a pool buffer without loading it: the reading half of an asynchronous load, callable from any thread and without the manager's lock held during the read.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `out` | receives the buffer. |
| Fails | `NotFound`, `Corrupt` (the size is not that of a blob), `LimitExceeded` (`assets.cache_bytes` or the pool's budget), `Io`. |
| Thread-safety | thread-safe. |
| Ownership | `*out` receives a share of the buffer. |

### `Unload(ResourceId,bool,Vector<ResourceId>*)` {#asset-resourcemanager-unload-resourceid-bool-vector-lt-resourceid-gt-star}

`method asset::ResourceManager::Unload` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_manager.h`

```cpp
Status Unload(ResourceId id, bool force = false, Vector<ResourceId>* dependents = nullptr);
```

Frees the resource `id` and the loaded resources that depend on it, when `force` is true (their handles go stale), or only when nothing loaded depends on it and no handle holds it.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `dependents` | receives the ids that stopped the call (`InUse`) or were unloaded (`force`); may be null. |
| Fails | `NotFound` (not loaded); `InUse` (a dependent is loaded, or a handle holds it, and `force` is false). |
| Ownership | `*dependents` is appended to. |

| Fails | `NotFound` (not loaded). |

### `InUseQuery()` {#asset-resourcemanager-inusequery}

`method asset::ResourceManager::InUseQuery` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_manager.h`

```cpp
ResourceInUseQuery InUseQuery();
```

The query to give `HostMemoryBackend::SetInUseQuery`, so the backend refuses to remove or replace a resource that something loaded depends on and a forced change unloads the dependents first. The manager must outlive the backend's use of it.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | never. |

### `PollChanges(Vector<ReloadResult>*)` {#asset-resourcemanager-pollchanges-vector-lt-reloadresult-gt-star}

`method asset::ResourceManager::PollChanges` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_manager.h`

```cpp
Status PollChanges(Vector<ReloadResult>* results);
```

Polls the mounts for changes and reloads the loaded resources that changed, each with its dependents, atomically: the new objects are made first (the closure rules are checked again against the new blob) and replace the old ones only when all succeeded; otherwise the old version stays loaded and the result says why. Raw pointers got from `Get` are valid until this call.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `results` | receives one entry per loaded resource that changed. |
| Fails | the failure of the mounts' `Watch`. |
| Ownership | `*results` is replaced. |

## `ProtoMessage` {#asset-protomessage}

`enum asset::ProtoMessage` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_protocol.h`

```cpp
enum class ProtoMessage : u8 { ... }
```

The kind of a message.

| Enumerator | Value | Description |
|---|---|---|
| <a id="asset-protomessage-hello"></a>`Hello` | `1` | A request: the version and the response size the client takes. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-stat"></a>`Stat` | `2` | A request: the size of a resource. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-table"></a>`Table` | `3` | A request: a page of the id and path table. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-list"></a>`List` | `4` | A request: a page of the ids under a path prefix. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-read"></a>`Read` | `5` | A request: a range of a resource. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-changes"></a>`Changes` | `6` | A request: what changed since an epoch. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-helloack"></a>`HelloAck` | `33` | A response: the version and the limits. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-statok"></a>`StatOk` | `34` | A response: the size. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-tableok"></a>`TableOk` | `35` | A response: a page of the table, or that it is unchanged. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-listok"></a>`ListOk` | `36` | A response: a page of ids. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-readok"></a>`ReadOk` | `37` | A response: the bytes. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-changesok"></a>`ChangesOk` | `38` | A response: the changes, or that the client must read the table again. |
| <a id="asset-protomessage-error"></a>`Error` | `63` | A response: a request failed. |

## `ProtoError` {#asset-protoerror}

`enum asset::ProtoError` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_protocol.h`

```cpp
enum class ProtoError : u8 { ... }
```

Why a request failed (the code of an `Error` message), or why a message could not be encoded or decoded.

| Enumerator | Value | Description |
|---|---|---|
| <a id="asset-protoerror-none"></a>`None` | `0` | No error. |
| <a id="asset-protoerror-notfound"></a>`NotFound` | `1` | The resource or the path prefix does not exist. |
| <a id="asset-protoerror-range"></a>`Range` | `2` | A read starts past the end of the resource. |
| <a id="asset-protoerror-toolarge"></a>`TooLarge` | `3` | A read asks for more than the server's limit, or a response would not fit. |
| <a id="asset-protoerror-busy"></a>`Busy` | `4` | The connection has more requests pending than the server takes at once: ask again. |
| <a id="asset-protoerror-invalid"></a>`Invalid` | `5` | The request is well formed but not valid (a page past the last). |
| <a id="asset-protoerror-unsupported"></a>`Unsupported` | `6` | The message is not a request this server answers. |
| <a id="asset-protoerror-other"></a>`Other` | `7` | The backend failed. |
| <a id="asset-protoerror-malformed"></a>`Malformed` | `8` | (Decoding only) the bytes are not a message of this protocol. |

## `EncodeProto(const ProtoData&,ByteSpan,MutableByteSpan,usize*)` {#asset-encodeproto-const-protodata-amp-bytespan-mutablebytespan-usize-star}

`function asset::EncodeProto` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_protocol.h`

```cpp
ProtoError EncodeProto(const ProtoData& message, ByteSpan payload, MutableByteSpan out, usize* written);
```

Encodes `message` into `out`.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `payload` | for a `ReadOk`, the bytes to carry; ignored for the other types. |
| Parameter `out` | the buffer; the message is written from its start. |
| Parameter `written` | receives the size of the message. |
| Returns | `None` on success. |
| Fails | `TooLarge` (the message does not fit `out`, or a path, a prefix or a page is above its limit), `Invalid` (a message type that has no encoding). |
| Ownership | `*written` is written on success; `payload` is read during the call only. |

## `EncodeProto(const ProtoData&,MutableByteSpan,usize*)` {#asset-encodeproto-const-protodata-amp-mutablebytespan-usize-star}

`function asset::EncodeProto` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_protocol.h`

```cpp
inline ProtoError EncodeProto(const ProtoData& message, MutableByteSpan out, usize* written);
```

As above for a message that carries no payload.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | as above. |
| Ownership | `*written` is written on success. |

## `DecodeProto(ByteSpan,u32,ProtoData*)` {#asset-decodeproto-bytespan-u32-protodata-star}

`function asset::DecodeProto` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_protocol.h`

```cpp
ProtoError DecodeProto(ByteSpan bytes, u32 max_read_bytes, ProtoData* out);
```

Decodes one message.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `bytes` | the message, exactly. |
| Parameter `out` | receives the message; replaced. |
| Parameter `max_read_bytes` | the longest run of bytes a `ReadOk` may carry and the largest `length` a `Read` may ask. |
| Returns | `None` on success. |
| Fails | `Malformed` for bytes that are not exactly one message (an unknown type, a truncation, a length above its limit, a second encoding of a value, bytes after the end); a request that is valid but above `max_read_bytes` is still decoded (the server answers `TooLarge`). |
| Ownership | `*out` is replaced; `bytes` is read during the call only. |

## `ResourceTable` {#asset-resourcetable}

`class asset::ResourceTable` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
class ResourceTable { ... }
```

A bidirectional map between normalized virtual paths and resource ids. One path names one id; one id may have any number of paths; one id has one type.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Thread-safety | thread-compatible; reads are safe from many threads while nothing mutates the table. |
| Ownership | owns its paths; views it returns are invalidated by any mutation. |

### `ResourceTable()` {#asset-resourcetable-resourcetable}

`constructor asset::ResourceTable::ResourceTable` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
ResourceTable();
```

Creates an empty table.

### `Add(StringView,ResourceId,ResourceType)` {#asset-resourcetable-add-stringview-resourceid-resourcetype}

`method asset::ResourceTable::Add` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
Status Add(StringView path, ResourceId id, ResourceType type);
```

Adds `path` for `id`, normalizing the path. Adding the same (path, id) again is accepted and changes nothing.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `Invalid` for a bad path or a null id or type; `DuplicateId` when the path already names another id; `TypeMismatch` when the id is known with another type; `LimitExceeded` above [`asset::kMaxTableEntries`](#asset-kmaxtableentries). |

### `Remove(StringView)` {#asset-resourcetable-remove-stringview}

`method asset::ResourceTable::Remove` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
bool Remove(StringView path);
```

Removes the entry for `path` (as written; it is normalized first). The cost is linear in the table size (the indexes are rebuilt), which suits tools and unmounting, not bulk removal in a loop.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Returns | true when an entry was removed. |
| Fails | never. |

### `Resolve(StringView,ResourceId*)` {#asset-resourcetable-resolve-stringview-resourceid-star-const}

`method asset::ResourceTable::Resolve` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
bool Resolve(StringView path, ResourceId* id) const;
```

Finds the id of `path` (as written; it is normalized first).

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `id` | receives the id; may be null. |
| Returns | false when the path is unknown or malformed. |
| Fails | never. |
| Ownership | `id` is written, never retained; `path` is not kept. |

### `TypeOf(ResourceId,ResourceType*)` {#asset-resourcetable-typeof-resourceid-resourcetype-star-const}

`method asset::ResourceTable::TypeOf` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
bool TypeOf(ResourceId id, ResourceType* type) const;
```

Finds the type of `id`.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `type` | receives the type; may be null. |
| Returns | false when no entry names the id. |
| Fails | never. |
| Ownership | `type` is written, never retained. |

### `PathsOf(ResourceId,Vector<StringView>*)` {#asset-resourcetable-pathsof-resourceid-vector-lt-stringview-gt-star-const}

`method asset::ResourceTable::PathsOf` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
usize PathsOf(ResourceId id, Vector<StringView>* out) const;
```

Appends every path of `id` to `out`, sorted.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Returns | the number appended. |
| Fails | never. |
| Ownership | the appended views borrow the table's strings until the table changes. |

### `ListPrefix(StringView,Vector<TableEntryView>*)` {#asset-resourcetable-listprefix-stringview-vector-lt-tableentryview-gt-star-const}

`method asset::ResourceTable::ListPrefix` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
usize ListPrefix(StringView prefix, Vector<TableEntryView>* out) const;
```

Appends every entry whose path starts with `prefix` to `out`, sorted by path.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Returns | the number appended. |
| Fails | never. |
| Ownership | the appended views borrow the table's strings until the table changes. |

`method asset::ResourceTable::Size` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
usize Size() const;
```

The number of entries (paths).

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | never. |

### `At(usize)` {#asset-resourcetable-at-usize-const}

`method asset::ResourceTable::At` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
TableEntryView At(usize index) const;
```

Returns entry `index` in insertion order.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Aborts | `index` is not below `Size()`. |

### `Merge(const ResourceTable&,Vector<ShadowedEntry>*)` {#asset-resourcetable-merge-const-resourcetable-amp-vector-lt-shadowedentry-gt-star}

`method asset::ResourceTable::Merge` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
Status Merge(const ResourceTable& lower, Vector<ShadowedEntry>* shadowed);
```

Merges a lower-priority table into this one: a path this table does not map is added; a path both map to the same id is kept; a path both map to different ids stays as this table has it and the other's entry is reported in `shadowed` (never dropped silently).

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `lower` | the lower-priority table. |
| Parameter `shadowed` | receives the entries that were not taken; may be null. |
| Fails | `TypeMismatch` when an id has one type here and another there (nothing is merged); `LimitExceeded` above [`asset::kMaxTableEntries`](#asset-kmaxtableentries) (nothing is merged). |
| Ownership | `shadowed` receives copies of the paths; `lower` is only read and not kept. |

### `WriteNamesBlob(const TypeRegistry&,Vector<byte>*)` {#asset-resourcetable-writenamesblob-const-typeregistry-amp-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-const}

`method asset::ResourceTable::WriteNamesBlob` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
Status WriteNamesBlob(const TypeRegistry& types, Vector<byte>* out) const;
```

Writes the table as a `NAMES` blob: one section of entries sorted by id then path, so the bytes depend only on the content.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | whatever `BlobWriter::Finish` reports. |
| Ownership | `out` receives the bytes; the table keeps no pointer to it. |

### `ReadNamesBlob(const BlobReader&)` {#asset-resourcetable-readnamesblob-const-blobreader-amp}

`method asset::ResourceTable::ReadNamesBlob` — defined in `engine/asset/resource_table.h`

```cpp
Status ReadNamesBlob(const BlobReader& blob);
```

Replaces this table with the content of a `NAMES` blob.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | `TypeMismatch` when the blob is not a `NAMES` blob; `Corrupt` for a damaged section or an entry that breaks the table's rules; the table is empty after a failure. |

## `BuildScriptBlob(const TypeRegistry&,ResourceId,const ScriptBuild&,Vector<byte>*,u32)` {#asset-buildscriptblob-const-typeregistry-amp-resourceid-const-scriptbuild-amp-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-u32}

`function asset::BuildScriptBlob` — defined in `engine/asset/script_blob.h`

```cpp
Status BuildScriptBlob(const TypeRegistry& types, ResourceId id, const ScriptBuild& build, Vector<byte>* out, u32 target = 0);
```

Builds a script blob.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `types` | the type registry; `SCRP` must be in it. |
| Parameter `id` | the script's id; not none. |
| Parameter `build` | the contents; the dependencies must not include `id` and must be strictly increasing. |
| Parameter `out` | replaced with the blob. |
| Parameter `target` | the target tag (0, development). |
| Fails | `Invalid` for a source that is empty or above the limit, a name or key over its limit, too many entries, a bytecode without a key (or a key without bytecode), or dependencies out of order. |
| Ownership | borrows the arguments for the call. |

## `ComputeInverseBindMatrices(Span<SkeletonJoint>)` {#asset-computeinversebindmatrices-span-lt-skeletonjoint-gt}

`function asset::ComputeInverseBindMatrices` — defined in `engine/asset/skeleton.h`

```cpp
void ComputeInverseBindMatrices(Span<SkeletonJoint> joints);
```

Fills every joint's `inverse_bind` from the bind pose: the product of the local matrices from the root down, inverted, computed in double precision and rounded to `f32` (deterministic).

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `joints` | parents before children; each parent index below its joint's. |
| Fails | never; a degenerate scale gives a non-finite matrix that [`asset::BuildSkeletonBlob`](#asset-buildskeletonblob-const-typeregistry-amp-resourceid-span-lt-const-skeletonjoint-gt-bool-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-u32) refuses. |

```cpp
ContentHash ComputeSkeletonHash(Span<const SkeletonJoint> joints);
```

The skeleton hash: 128 bits over the joint count, the parents and the names (what a clip or a skinned mesh records to prove it was made for this skeleton).

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Fails | never. |

## `BuildSkeletonBlob(const TypeRegistry&,ResourceId,Span<const SkeletonJoint>,bool,Vector<byte>*,u32)` {#asset-buildskeletonblob-const-typeregistry-amp-resourceid-span-lt-const-skeletonjoint-gt-bool-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-u32}

`function asset::BuildSkeletonBlob` — defined in `engine/asset/skeleton.h`

```cpp
Status BuildSkeletonBlob(const TypeRegistry& types, ResourceId id, Span<const SkeletonJoint> joints, bool include_names, Vector<byte>* out, u32 target = 0);
```

Writes the blob of a skeleton, deterministically.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `types` | the registry; it must hold `kSkeleton`. |
| Parameter `id` | the skeleton's resource id. |
| Parameter `joints` | at most `kMaxSkeletonJoints`, parents before children, unique non-empty names of at most `kMaxJointNameBytes`, finite values, unit rotations with w >= 0, non-zero scales, finite inverse bind matrices. |
| Parameter `include_names` | whether the name hashes and names go into the blob (the skeleton hash covers them either way). |
| Fails | `Invalid` naming the first broken rule and the joint; `LimitExceeded` above the joint limit (the message names the limit); the errors of [`asset::BlobWriter::Finish`](#asset-blobwriter-finish-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-const). |
| Ownership | `out` receives the bytes; nothing is borrowed after the call. |

## `ErrorCode` {#asset-errorcode}

`enum asset::ErrorCode` — defined in `engine/asset/status.h`

```cpp
enum class ErrorCode : u8 { ... }
```

What went wrong.

| Enumerator | Value | Description |
|---|---|---|
| <a id="asset-errorcode-ok"></a>`Ok` |  | Success. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-missingdependency"></a>`MissingDependency` |  | A resource names a dependency that no mount, table or pak holds. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-dependencytypemismatch"></a>`DependencyTypeMismatch` |  | A dependency exists but is not of the type (or the version) the reference declared. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-dependencycycle"></a>`DependencyCycle` |  | Resources depend on each other in a cycle. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-duplicateid"></a>`DuplicateId` |  | Two resources (or two paths, or two sources) claim one id, or one path claims two ids. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-notfound"></a>`NotFound` |  | A named thing does not exist: an id, a path, a section. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-corrupt"></a>`Corrupt` |  | Bytes are damaged: truncated, out of range, overlapping, not aligned, or a hash that does not match. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-unsupportedversion"></a>`UnsupportedVersion` |  | A blob, pak or container is not the current version; the message names `asset_mint remint`. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-typemismatch"></a>`TypeMismatch` |  | A resource is not of the type the caller asked for, or the type is not registered. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-toolarge"></a>`TooLarge` |  | A size or count is above a bound. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-io"></a>`Io` |  | The medium failed: a read or write error. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-cancelled"></a>`Cancelled` |  | The operation was cancelled and changed nothing that was committed. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-offline"></a>`Offline` |  | The network backend is offline and the cache does not hold the resource. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-limitexceeded"></a>`LimitExceeded` |  | A configured limit (a budget, a count) is exceeded. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-wrongtarget"></a>`WrongTarget` |  | A blob or pak was made for another target platform; the message names `asset_mint pack --target`. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-invalid"></a>`Invalid` |  | The caller broke a rule: a malformed path, a null id where one is required. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-inuse"></a>`InUse` |  | A resource cannot be replaced or removed because a loaded resource depends on it. |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-notready"></a>`NotReady` |  | An answer needs a table that has not arrived yet (not the same as `NotFound`). |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-unsupported"></a>`Unsupported` |  | A file uses a feature this version does not import (the importer names it). |
| <a id="asset-errorcode-other"></a>`Other` |  | Anything else. |

## `BuildTextureBlob(const TypeRegistry&,ResourceId,const TextureBuildInput&,Vector<byte>*,u32)` {#asset-buildtextureblob-const-typeregistry-amp-resourceid-const-texturebuildinput-amp-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-u32}

| Ownership | borrows `input` for the call; `*out` is replaced. |

## `MintTextureFromPixels(const TypeRegistry&,ResourceId,const TextureMintSpec&,u32,u32,Span<const ByteSpan>,Vector<byte>*,u32,jobs::Scheduler*)` {#asset-minttexturefrompixels-const-typeregistry-amp-resourceid-const-texturemintspec-amp-u32-u32-span-lt-const-bytespan-gt-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-u32-jobs-scheduler-star}

`function asset::MintTextureFromPixels` — defined in `engine/asset/texture_mint.h`

```cpp
Status MintTextureFromPixels(const TypeRegistry& types, ResourceId id, const TextureMintSpec& spec, u32 width, u32 height, Span<const ByteSpan> images, Vector<byte>* out, u32 target = 0, jobs::Scheduler* scheduler = nullptr);
```

Mints a texture blob from tightly packed RGBA8 layers.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `types` | the registry; it must hold `TEXR`. |
| Parameter `id` | the texture's id. |
| Parameter `spec` | kind, format, color space, mips, filter, alpha. |
| Parameter `width` | texels across (1 to 16384). |
| Parameter `height` | texels down. |
| Parameter `images` | one `width * height * 4` byte layer per layer of the kind (1, 6 for a cube, any for an array). |
| Parameter `out` | replaced by the blob. |
| Parameter `target` | the API layout of the blob (0 for the default). |
| Parameter `scheduler` | runs the block compression on workers when not null. |
| Fails | `Invalid` (a layer of another size, a cube that is not square, a mip count above the full chain), `LimitExceeded` (memory), and what [`asset::BuildTextureBlob`](#asset-buildtextureblob-const-typeregistry-amp-resourceid-const-texturebuildinput-amp-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-u32) reports. |
| Ownership | borrows its arguments for the call only; `*out` is replaced. |

## `MintTextureFromHalfPixels(const TypeRegistry&,ResourceId,const TextureMintSpec&,u32,u32,Span<const ByteSpan>,Vector<byte>*,u32)` {#asset-minttexturefromhalfpixels-const-typeregistry-amp-resourceid-const-texturemintspec-amp-u32-u32-span-lt-const-bytespan-gt-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-u32}

`function asset::MintTextureFromHalfPixels` — defined in `engine/asset/texture_mint.h`

```cpp
Status MintTextureFromHalfPixels(const TypeRegistry& types, ResourceId id, const TextureMintSpec& spec, u32 width, u32 height, Span<const ByteSpan> images, Vector<byte>* out, u32 target = 0);
```

Mints a texture blob from tightly packed half-float RGBA layers (a high-dynamic-range image).

The format is `RGBA16_Float`, the mip chain is filtered in exact integer arithmetic like the 8-bit one, and nothing is converted.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `types` | the registry; it must hold `TEXR`. |
| Parameter `id` | the texture's id. |
| Parameter `spec` | kind, mips, filter; `format` must be `RGBA16_Float` and `alpha` must not be `Premultiply`; with `environment` (a cube) the levels from 1 are the GGX specular chain ([`asset::PrefilterEnvironmentChains`](#asset-prefilterenvironmentchains-u32-u32-vector-lt-vector-lt-byte-gt-gt-star)). |
| Parameter `width` | texels across (1 to 16384). |
| Parameter `height` | texels down. |
| Parameter `images` | one `width * height * 8` byte layer per layer of the kind; every texel finite. |
| Parameter `out` | replaced by the blob. |
| Parameter `target` | the API layout of the blob (0 for the default). |
| Fails | `Invalid` (another format, a layer of another size, a cube that is not square, a mip count above the full chain), `Corrupt` for an infinite or NaN texel, `LimitExceeded` (memory), and what [`asset::BuildTextureBlob`](#asset-buildtextureblob-const-typeregistry-amp-resourceid-const-texturebuildinput-amp-vector-lt-byte-gt-star-u32) reports. |
| Ownership | borrows its arguments for the call only; `*out` is replaced. |

## `MipAlpha` {#asset-mipalpha}

`enum asset::MipAlpha` — defined in `engine/asset/texture_mips.h`

```cpp
enum class MipAlpha : u32 { ... }
```

How the alpha channel is treated while filtering.

| Enumerator | Value | Description |
|---|---|---|
| <a id="asset-mipalpha-straight"></a>`Straight` | `0` | 8-bit RGBA only: colors are weighted by alpha while filtering and stored straight. |
| <a id="asset-mipalpha-premultiply"></a>`Premultiply` | `1` | The level 0 colors were multiplied by alpha (`PremultiplyAlphaRgba8`); all levels are filtered as plain values and stay premultiplied. |
| <a id="asset-mipalpha-ignore"></a>`Ignore` | `2` | Every channel is filtered as it is. |

## `ConvertRgba8(rhi::Format,bool,u32,u32,ByteSpan,Vector<byte>*)` {#asset-convertrgba8-rhi-format-bool-u32-u32-bytespan-vector-lt-byte-gt-star}

`function asset::ConvertRgba8` — defined in `engine/asset/texture_mips.h`

```cpp
Status ConvertRgba8(rhi::Format format, bool source_srgb, u32 width, u32 height, ByteSpan rgba, Vector<byte>* out);
```

Converts 8-bit RGBA texels into `format`: `R8_UNorm` takes red, `RG8_UNorm` red and green, `RGBA8_*` copies, and the half-float formats take linear values (sRGB color channels are decoded when `source_srgb`; alpha is always linear).

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `format` | a supported format. |
| Parameter `source_srgb` | whether the color channels of `rgba` are sRGB-encoded. |
| Parameter `rgba` | `width * height` texels. |
| Parameter `out` | replaced by the converted texels, tightly packed. |
| Fails | `Invalid` for an unsupported format or a wrong size; `LimitExceeded` when memory runs out. |
| Ownership | borrows `rgba` for the call; `*out` is replaced. |

## `GenerateMipChain(rhi::Format,u32,u32,ByteSpan,const MipOptions&,Vector<byte>*)` {#asset-generatemipchain-rhi-format-u32-u32-bytespan-const-mipoptions-amp-vector-lt-byte-gt-star}

`function asset::GenerateMipChain` — defined in `engine/asset/texture_mips.h`

```cpp
Status GenerateMipChain(rhi::Format format, u32 width, u32 height, ByteSpan level0, const MipOptions& options, Vector<byte>* chain);
```

Makes the full mip chain of an image: level 0 is copied, then each level is filtered from the one above.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Parameter `format` | a supported format. |
| Parameter `width` | 1 to [`asset::kMaxTextureSize`](#asset-kmaxtexturesize). |
| Parameter `height` | 1 to [`asset::kMaxTextureSize`](#asset-kmaxtexturesize). |
| Parameter `level0` | `TightLevelBytes(format, width, height)` bytes. |
| Parameter `options` | the filter and alpha treatment (`Straight` needs `RGBA8_*`). |
| Parameter `chain` | replaced by all levels, level 0 first, each tightly packed. |
| Fails | `Invalid` for an unsupported format, a wrong size, `Straight` on a format without 8-bit alpha, or a half-float texel that is infinite or NaN; `LimitExceeded` when memory runs out. |
| Ownership | borrows `level0` for the call; `*chain` is replaced. |
